### [1] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaboration for Masonry Construction Under Material and Assembly Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20264)
*Jutang Gao,Arash Adel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向砌体施工的人机协作自适应工作流程，通过末端投影引导和激光扫描反馈修正，有效解决了人机通信受限和公差累积问题，在砖墙实验中提升了胶粘剂涂抹一致性和放置精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑施工中人机协作面临机器人向人类通信受限，以及材料和装配不确定性导致的公差累积两大挑战，现有方法缺乏有效应对手段。

Method: 设计了两种互补机制：1）安装在机器人末端的投影仪提供空间配准的实时投影引导，辅助人工涂抹胶粘剂；2）激光扫描实现基于反馈的抓取和放置位姿修正，自适应调整人和机器人的动作以应对材料变异和公差累积。

Result: 全尺寸实验（常规顺砖及非标准构型）表明：投影引导提高了胶粘剂涂抹一致性并缩短了作业时间；激光修正保持了砖层水平，避免了开环执行引发的碰撞故障。

Conclusion: 融合空间投影与基于传感反馈的自适应机制，能够缓解公差累积，提升人机协作建筑施工的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration in construction is often challenged by limited robot-to-human communication and the need to adapt to tolerance accumulation arising from material and assembly uncertainties. We present an adaptive human-robot collaborative workflow for masonry construction that addresses communication limitations and tolerance accumulation, demonstrated through a brickwork case study in which a robot places bricks while a human applies adhesive. This workflow is enabled by two complementary mechanisms: 1) an end-effector-mounted projector that provides spatially registered, just-in-time projection guidance for manual adhesive application, and 2) laser scanning for feedback-driven grasping and placement pose correction. Together, these mechanisms enable adjustment of human and robotic actions in response to material variability and accumulated assembly tolerances. Full-scale experiments across conventional running-bond and nonstandard configurations demonstrate that projection guidance improves adhesive application consistency and reduces application time, while laser-based correction maintains level courses and avoids collision-prone failures associated with open-loop execution. These results indicate that integrating spatial projection with feedback-driven adaptation, enabled by material and as-built sensing, can mitigate tolerance accumulation and improve precision and robustness in human-robot collaborative construction.

</details>


### [2] [Terrestrial Soft Mobile Robots: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20304)
*Dimuthu D. K. Arachchige*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了无轮式软体陆地移动机器人的研究现状，涵盖运动策略、驱动方法、建模方法与控制系统，并指出了未来关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体移动机器人在搜救、服务、安防、探测和制造等领域具有广阔应用前景，但目前缺乏对其无轮式陆地移动系统的系统性综述，亟需梳理现状并明确发展方向。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统回顾了软体移动机器人在运动策略、驱动方式、建模方法和控制系统方面的过去与当前发展，并归纳了关键研究挑战。

Result: 全面总结了无轮式软体陆地移动机器人的各类运动模式（如爬行、跳跃、蠕动等）及其对应的驱动和建模方案，识别了阻碍其广泛部署的核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该综述为软体移动机器人领域的研究者和从业者提供了系统性参考，强调了解决当前技术挑战对于推动实际应用落地的重要性。

Abstract: Soft mobile robots have emerged as a promising area of research with potential applications in various disciplines including but not limited to search-and-rescue, service, surveillance, explorations, and manufacturing. In this article, we provide a comprehensive review of the current state of soft mobile robot research, focusing on wheelless terrestrial locomotive systems. We include past and present developments in locomotion strategies, actuation methods, modeling approaches, and control systems. Further, we identify key research challenges that must be overcome to enable the widespread adoption of soft mobile robots in various applications. Overall, this article provides a valuable resource for researchers and practitioners interested in the field of soft mobile robots and soft robotics.

</details>


### [3] [Proximal State Nudging: Reducing Skill Atrophy from AI Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20355)
*Megha Srivastava,Jonathan Ouyang,Eric Zhou,Andrew Silva,Emily Sumner,Dorsa Sadigh,Yuchen Cui,Deepak Gopinath,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出近端状态引导（PSN）算法，在半自主系统中通过状态引导联合优化技能发展与任务表现，模拟实验和人类受试者研究（n=60）表明，PSN比现有基线在技能提升和任务表现间取得更好平衡，在无人辅助技能上提升高达7倍，同时碰撞减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半自主共享控制系统中，技能退化（Skill Atrophy）构成安全风险，操作员可能无法区分自身输入与自主修正，导致能力逐渐下降。现有方法缺乏对学习兼容性的考虑，亟需一种既能提升任务表现又能促进人类技能发展的共享自主算法。

Method: 提出Proximal State Nudging (PSN)算法，通过将用户"轻推"到估计最可学习的状态，联合优化技能发展和任务表现。首先在LunarLander环境用模拟学生进行验证，随后在CARLA模拟器中开展两项驾驶任务（高性能赛车和侧方停车，n=60）的人类受试者研究。

Result: PSN在平衡学生提升与共享表现上优于现有基线；人类实验中，PSN产生高达7倍的无辅助技能增益，比无辅助自练习减少50%碰撞。

Conclusion: PSN是首个纳入学习兼容性考虑的共享自主规划器，实验证明通过状态引导能够有效缓解技能退化，在不牺牲安全性的前提下促进人类技能发展。

Abstract: Skill atrophy, the gradual decline of human capability under AI assistance, poses a safety risk in shared-control of semi-autonomous systems, where operators may be unable to distinguish their own inputs from autonomous corrections. We propose Proximal State Nudging (PSN), a shared autonomy algorithm that jointly optimizes for skill development and task performance by nudging users toward states estimated to be most learnable. We first show that PSN outperforms existing shared autonomy baselines in balancing student improvement in unassisted reward with overall shared performance, using simulated students in the classic LunarLander environment. We then present, to the best of our knowledge, the first human subject studies of a planner incorporating learning-compatible shared autonomy: across two driving tasks in the CARLA simulator (High Performance Racing and Parallel Parking, n = 60), PSN produces up to 7x larger gains in unassisted skill than standard blended shared autonomy, while incurring 50% fewer collisions than unassisted self-practice.

</details>


### [4] [SUGAR: A Scalable Human-Video-Driven Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20373)
*Tianshu Wu,Xiangqi Kong,Yue Chen,Qize Yu,Hang Ye,Jia Li,Yizhou Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUGAR是一个可扩展的数据驱动框架，通过从人类视频中提取运动先验并经过物理精炼和策略蒸馏，实现人形机器人全身移动操作技能的零样本真实世界部署，无需任务特定奖励工程或参考运动条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人全身移动操作方法存在任务特定奖励工程繁琐、参考运动泛化性差、遥操作成本高等问题。人类视频数据虽丰富但存在遮挡、接触伪影和重定向误差，难以直接用于策略学习。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 从非结构化人类视频中自动提取运动轨迹和接触标签等运动学交互先验；(2) 基于特权的物理精炼器，使用统一模仿奖励和渐进状态池将不完美先验转化为物理可行的技能；(3) 将精炼技能蒸馏为包含命令生成器和命令跟踪器的分层自主策略。

Result: 在六个代表性移动操作任务的仿真和真实人形机器人上，SUGAR显著优于参考跟踪基线，性能随人类视频数据量增加而提升，实现了零样本真实世界迁移、闭环执行、自主故障恢复和稳定的长时程性能。

Conclusion: SUGAR证明了从大量人类视频中学习可泛化的人形机器人移动操作技能是可行的，为摆脱人工奖励工程和遥操作瓶颈提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Building humanoid robots capable of generalizable whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods either rely on laborious task-specific reward engineering, rigidly replay reference motions that fail to generalize, or depend on costly teleoperation that limits scalability. While human videos capture diverse human behaviors, motion priors inferred from them are inherently imperfect, suffering from occlusion, contact artifacts, and retargeting errors that render them unsuitable for direct policy learning. To address this, we present SUGAR, a scalable data-driven framework that converts diverse human videos into deployable humanoid loco-manipulation skills, without any task-specific reward engineering or reference-motion conditioning at inference. SUGAR proceeds in three stages. First, a fully automated pipeline extracts kinematic interaction priors including human-object motion trajectories and contact labels from unstructured human videos. Second, a privileged physics-based refiner uses a unified mimic reward and progressive state pool to transform imperfect priors into physically feasible, high-fidelity skills. Third, refined skills are distilled into a hierarchical autonomous policy consisting of a command generator and a command tracker. We evaluate SUGAR on six representative loco-manipulation tasks in simulation and real-world humanoid hardware. Our method substantially outperforms reference-tracking baselines, and performance scales clearly with the amount of human video data. It also achieves zero-shot real-world transfer with reliable closed-loop execution, autonomous failure recovery, and stable long-horizon performance under external perturbations. Project Page: https://tianshuwu.github.io/sugar-humanoid/

</details>


### [5] [Scalable Multi-robot Motion Planning via Hierarchical Subproblem Expansion and Workspace Decomposition Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20395)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用工作空间分解的离散搜索来协调多机器人运动规划的方法，将规划时间提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划面临的根本挑战是如何在避免机器人间冲突的同时，避免搜索联合配置空间带来的巨大计算开销。

Method: 通过工作空间分解进行离散搜索来提供机器人间的协调，并迭代细化工作空间表示，使得规划器能够搜索更小、解耦的配置空间。

Result: 规划时间提升高达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 迭代细化工作空间表示可以有效协调多机器人运动规划，显著降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning is achieving sufficient coordination to avoid inter-robot conflicts without incurring the large computational expense of searching the joint configuration space of the robot group. In this work, we present a method for multiple mobile robot motion planning that achieves an improvement in planning time up to an order of magnitude by leveraging the insight that we can use discrete search over a workspace decomposition to provide coordination between robots during planning. While prior work uses workspace topology to inform when coordination between robots is needed and then composes robots into their joint configuration space, we take a step further by iteratively refining our workspace representation to allow our planner to search smaller, decoupled configuration spaces.

</details>


### [6] [Enhancing Graph-Based SLAM in GNSS-Denied environments by leveraging leg odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20484)
*Léon Perruchot-Triboulet,Luc Jaulin,Kai Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种因子图架构，在LIO-SAM框架中增加基于腿部里程计的并行运动学支路，通过带选择性噪声模型的恒等相对位姿约束与LiDAR-惯性主支路耦合，在GNSS拒止环境下将四足机器人的高程漂移从30米以上降至30厘米以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决足式机器人在GNSS拒止环境中导航时，外感知传感器（如LiDAR）在几何稀疏或重复场景下存在高程漂移的问题。

Method: 在LIO-SAM因子图框架中引入并行运动学支路（基于本体感觉的腿部里程计），通过恒等相对位姿约束（带选择性噪声模型）将其与主LiDAR-惯性支路耦合。

Result: 在Linxai D50四足平台上进行总计超1公里的两段户外闭环实验，高程漂移从超过30米降至30厘米以下，且在基线管线完全失败的场景中实现了收敛。

Conclusion: 本体感觉数据（已在线计算用于步态控制）可作为GNSS拒止环境中SLAM的一种轻量级且有效的垂直锚点。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied environments remains a core challenge for legged robots, where exteroceptive sensors such as LiDAR are prone to elevation drift in geometrically sparse or repetitive scenes. We present a factor graph architecture that augments the LIO-SAM framework with a parallel kinematic lane driven by proprioceptive leg odometry, coupled to the main LiDAR-inertial lane via an identity relative pose constraint with a selective noise model. Applied to a Linxai D50 quadruped platform across two outdoor loops totaling over one kilometer, our approach reduces elevation drift from over 30m to under 30cm and enables convergence in a scene where the baseline pipeline fails entirely. These results suggest that proprioceptive data, already computed onboard for gait control, constitutes a lightweight and effective vertical anchor for SLAM in GNSS-denied settings.

</details>


### [7] [The Yes-Man Syndrome: Benchmarking Abstention in Embodied Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20544)
*Doguhan Yeke,Elif Su Temirel,Ananth Shreekumar,Brandon Lee,Dongyan Xu,Z Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboAbstention框架，用于评估和提高视觉-语言模型在具身机器人场景中的"弃权"能力（即识别并拒绝执行模糊、不可行或基于错误前提的指令）。通过对多个前沿VLM的测试发现，即使最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash的弃权率仅为39.0%，但通过防御性提示和上下文学习可提升至93.6%，但问题仍未完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM弃权研究主要针对纯文本场景，未能捕捉具身机器人环境中固有的感知基础和物理约束。在具身场景中，弃权需要识别模糊、物理不可行、基于错误前提或无法通过当前感知模态解决的指令，而缺乏专门针对这一需求的基准数据集和评估框架。

Method: 提出RoboAbstention框架，包含：(1)结构化视觉基础（structured visual grounding），(2)确定性约束推导（deterministic constraint derivation），(3)通过类别特定模板进行受控指令生成（controlled instruction generation via category-specific templates）。基于五个机器人数据集图像生成带有可验证弃权条件的数据集。

Result: 构建包含6,069条基准指令的数据集。评估多个前沿VLM，最佳模型Gemini 2.5 Flash弃权率仅39.0%，Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview仅16.5%。通过防御性提示和上下文学习，Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview弃权率提升至93.6%，GPT 5.4 Mini达88.6%，但未能完全解决问题。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在具身机器人场景中的弃权能力存在显著缺陷，即使具备高级推理能力的模型也表现不佳。防御性提示和上下文学习等干预措施可大幅提升弃权率，但尚无方法能完全解决该问题，表明该方向仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are used as high-level planners for embodied agents, translating natural language instructions and visual observations into action plans. While prior work has studied abstention in LLMs, existing benchmarks are largely text-only and do not capture the perceptual grounding and physical constraints inherent to embodied robotics environments. In such settings, abstention requires recognizing when instructions are ambiguous, physically infeasible, based on false premises, or otherwise unresolvable given the available sensory modalities and context. To address this gap, we introduce a taxonomy to categorize abstention in the context of embodied robotics and present RoboAbstention, a scalable and auditable framework for generating abstention instructions grounded in images gathered from five robotics datasets. RoboAbstention instantiates the taxonomy through a three-phase pipeline: (1) structured visual grounding, (2) deterministic constraint derivation, and (3) controlled instruction generation via category-specific templates. This enables the construction of a diverse dataset with verifiable abstention conditions. We evaluate several frontier VLMs and find that all models exhibit significant weaknesses in abstention, including those with advanced reasoning capabilities. The best-performing model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, abstains on only 39.0% of our 6,069 benchmark instructions, while the embodied planner Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview abstains on just 16.5%. We further explore methods for improving abstention in VLM planners, such as defensive prompting and in-context learning, and find that these interventions substantially improve performance, reaching 93.6% abstention rate for Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview and 88.6% for GPT 5.4 Mini, yet no approach fully solves the problem. We open-source RoboAbstention at https://purseclab.github.io/RoboAbstention/.

</details>


### [8] [Fault-Tolerant, Rigidity-Preserving Control of Inflatable Truss Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20561)
*James Wade,Isaac Weaver,Mihai Stanciu,Nathan Usevitch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对充气式机器人桁架的容错控制框架，通过扩展运动学优化、引入离散时间控制障碍函数约束和闭环位置控制，在电机故障下仍能保持功能，实验表明单电机故障时工作空间保留>69%，跟踪精度提升>25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周机器人桁架在操作环境中可能发生电机故障，严重限制其操作能力，现有方法未充分解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 扩展运动学优化以处理任意组合的电机故障，通过等式约束确保故障执行器不被使用；2) 引入离散时间控制障碍函数(DTCBF)约束，在保证结构刚性的同时最大化工作空间利用；3) 使用机载编码器反馈和基于正运动学的状态估计器实现闭环位置控制。

Result: 在2D六执行器等周桁架测试平台上，单电机故障下工作空间保留>69%，闭环控制跟踪精度提升>25%。

Conclusion: 该方法为在驱动退化条件下运行的等周桁架机器人奠定了更鲁棒和更 resilient 的基础。

Abstract: Isoperimetric robotic trusses can adapt to different tasks and environments because they have a high strength-to-weight ratio, can change their own shape dramatically, and can be reconfigured into a variety of different shapes. However, motor failures in operational environments can severely limit operational capabilities if not properly addressed. This paper presents a fault-tolerant control framework for an inflatable robotic truss that maintains functionality despite motor failures, shown through three key contributions. First, we extend the kinematic optimization to handle arbitrary combinations of motor failures by imposing equality constraints to ensure failed actuators are not used. Second, we introduce discrete-time control barrier function (DTCBF) constraints that mathematically guarantee structural rigidity while maximizing workspace utilization, a critical requirement for reliable operation of truss robots under discrete-time control. Third, we implement closed-loop position control using onboard encoder feedback and a forward kinematics-based state estimator, improving positional accuracy in the presence of disturbances. We validate our approach through simulation and hardware experiments on a 2D isoperimetric truss testbed. For a 2D configuration with 6 actuators, we demonstrate >69% workspace preservation under single-motor failures and a >25% improvement in tracking accuracy with closed-loop control. These results establish a foundation for more robust and resilient isoperimetric truss robots operating under degraded actuation.

</details>


### [9] [Conflict-Aware Active Perception and Control in 3D Gaussian Splatting Fields via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20566)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Vivek Pandey,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种冲突感知主动感知与控制框架，在3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）环境中平衡安全探索与信息采集，通过CBF约束安全、风险感知EIG选择最佳视角、感知势函数对齐相机方向，并利用松弛变量的二次规划协调安全与感知的冲突目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动感知中安全导航与信息采集目标存在固有冲突，因为高信息视角常位于高不确定性的危险区域，现有3DGS方法无法有效平衡二者。

Method: 1) 基于平均风险价值（AV@R）碰撞风险指标构建控制障碍函数（CBF）保证安全前向不变性；2) 提出风险感知的期望信息增益（EIG）用于下一最佳视角选择；3) 设计感知势函数使相机朝向局部信息上升方向；4) 构建统一的安全关键、感知感知二次规划，安全为硬约束，感知约束引入松弛变量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与现有3DGS方法相比，所提方法在安全性和信息采集方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的冲突感知框架能有效协调安全与感知的冲突目标，提升机器人在不确定3DGS环境中的自主探索性能。

Abstract: Active perception in uncertain environments requires robots to navigate safely while acquiring informative observations to reduce map uncertainty. These objectives inherently conflict, as informative viewpoints often lie near uncertain regions with higher collision risk. To address this challenge, we develop a conflict-aware active perception and control framework for robotic systems operating in environments represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Safety is enforced using a Control Barrier Function (CBF) derived from an Average Value-at-Risk AV@R collision-risk metric that accounts for geometric uncertainty and guarantees forward invariance of a safe set. To improve perception, we propose a risk-aware Expected Information Gain (EIG) formulation for selecting the next-best-view and introduce perception barrier functions that align the camera orientation with the local information-ascent direction. To obtain a tractable formulation for these conflicting safety and perception objectives, we propose a unified safety-critical, perception-aware quadratic program that enforces safety as a hard constraint while relaxing perception constraints through slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves both safety and information acquisition compared to existing 3DGS-based approaches.

</details>


### [10] [Intent-First Aerial V2V for Tactical Coordination and Separation: Protocol and Performance Under Density and Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20595)
*Mehrnaz Sabet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并表征了面向密集低空无人机交通管理（UTM）的、全空中、直连链路级、意图优先的V2V战术邻域交换协议栈，融合刷新状态与意图信标及事件触发消息，实现局部感知、协同感知、降级评估与资源共享协调。基于C-V2X模块实现，通过高压力场景测试验证其在中低密度下有效，高密度下转为有保护回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集低空飞行需要战术间隔管理这一中间层，但现有方案缺乏可大规模部署的邻域通信机制来提供新鲜、可信的局部协调信息。

Method: 设计并实现全空中、直连链路级、意图优先的V2V战术邻域交换协议栈，包含刷新状态/意图信标（局部感知、协同感知、降级评估）与事件触发消息（让行、排序、释放、应急协调），基于C-V2X直连链路模块并加入认证新鲜度检查，通过实时场测锚定的高压力场景测试验证。

Result: V2V协议栈能减少陈旧信念发散、通过协同感知保持可观测性、拒绝无效战术消息、抑制虚假局部推断、结构化共享资源协调。在中低密度下可行，但在密度、干扰和复杂度增加时转向有保护回退。

Conclusion: 意图优先的空中V2V是密集低空无人机战术间隔协调的可边界赋能器，为在扰动驱动的城市空域中扩展战术协调提供了可行的通信层基础。

Abstract: Dense low-altitude aerial operations require more than pre-flight route coordination and last-resort collision avoidance. Once aircraft are airborne, disturbances can emerge on timescales shorter than strategic reauthorization can absorb, while collision avoidance is too late and disruptive to serve as routine traffic management. Although tactical separation is recognized as the intermediate layer, realizing it at scale requires a deployable neighborhood communication mechanism that provides fresh, trusted information for local coordination. This paper presents what is, to our knowledge, the first controller-coupled characterization of an all-airborne, sidelink-class, intent-first vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) tactical neighborhood exchange stack for dense Unmanned Aircraft System Traffic Management (UTM) operations. Unlike awareness-only broadcast, the proposed exchange combines refreshed state and intent beacons for local awareness, cooperative perception, and degraded-mode assessment with event-triggered messages for yielding, sequencing, release, and contingency coordination. We implement and evaluate this model on an all-airborne V2V stack using sidelink-class C-V2X modules with authenticated freshness checks. Evaluation uses a scenario-driven, high-volume stress campaign supported by real-time, field-anchored infrastructure. Results show that V2V reduces stale-belief divergence, preserves observability through cooperative perception, rejects invalid tactical messages, suppresses false local inference, and structures shared-resource coordination. The implemented stack provides a viable communication layer for tactical separation in lower-to-moderate regimes, but transitions toward guarded fallback as density, impairment, and complexity increase. These findings position intent-first aerial V2V as a bounded enabler for scaling tactical coordination in disturbance-driven urban airspace.

</details>


### [11] [Jointly Learning Predicates and Actions Enables Zero-Shot Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20648)
*Benedict Quartey,Sebastian Castro,Eric Rosen,Wil Thomason,George Konidaris,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Predicate Action Skills (PACTS)，一种联合建模动作轨迹与谓词信念轨迹的闭环视触觉策略，通过谓词作为符号接口实现技能的零样本组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法学到的技能无法泛化到已知技能的新组合，且生成式策略仅建模动作轨迹分布，无法推理实现鲁棒组合所需的符号化结果。

Method: 提出PACTS，在封闭循环的视触觉策略中联合生成动作轨迹和谓词信念轨迹，使单个模型同时输出连贯的动作与符号化结果，并利用在线谓词预测作为符号接口进行规划与执行监控。

Result: 联合生成动作与谓词使PACTS学到更好的内部表征，同时提升动作生成和谓词分类性能；通过谓词预测实现学得技能的零样本组合。

Conclusion: PACTS通过将符号化推理融入生成式策略，有效实现了机器人技能的零样本组合泛化。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn complex behaviors from expert examples, yet existing approaches often fail to generalize to new compositions of known skills without retraining. Modern generative policies model distributions over action trajectories alone, thus are unable to reason about the symbolic outcomes required for robust composition. We propose that skills should jointly model action trajectories and the symbolic outcomes they induce. To address this gap, we introduce Predicate Action Skills (PACTS), a class of closed-loop visuomotor policies that model skills as a joint generative process over action and predicate belief trajectories, producing coherent action-outcome rollouts within a single model. Jointly generating actions and predicates enables PACTS to learn internal representations that improve both action generation and predicate classification. Furthermore, we demonstrate zero-shot composition of learned skills via planning by leveraging online predicate predictions from PACTS as a symbolic interface for sequencing and monitoring execution. Project website: https://planpacts.github.io/

</details>


### [12] [A Semantic and Occlusion-Aware GM-PHD Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20666)
*Jovan Menezes,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合深度学习语义信息与遮挡感知机制的新生模型，用于构建遮挡感知的高斯混合概率假设密度（GM-PHD）滤波器。该模型通过显式考虑遮挡区域并利用环境语义信息定义初始化项，解决了传统方法中新生假设过于简化的问题，在复杂高密度驾驶场景中提升了跟踪性能。基于KITTI数据集的蒙特卡洛仿真实验表明，所提方法在遮挡严重场景下可减少初始化延迟，约70%情况下达到或超越最强基线，并提供了新生模型权重的敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯跟踪框架中新生模型通常基于简单或均匀假设，未能充分利用环境语义信息与遮挡结构，导致在复杂高密度驾驶场景下目标检测与跟踪的初始化延迟高、精度低。

Method: 提出语义遮挡感知（S-OA）新生模型，将深度学习导出的语义信息与遮挡区域显式建模融入GM-PHD滤波器的新生初始化项中，使滤波器能够更准确地表征新目标最可能出现的位置。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上的蒙特卡洛仿真实验显示，S-OA新生模型在遮挡严重场景下降低了初始化延迟，约70%情况下匹配或超越最强基线；同时通过均值绝对基数误差和OSPA指标验证了跟踪精度提升。

Conclusion: 将遮挡推理与语义先验融入贝叶斯跟踪框架能够显著提升自动驾驶场景下的跟踪性能，验证了语义信息与遮挡感知在GM-PHD滤波器中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new birth model including semantic information derived from deep learning to create an occlusion-aware Gaussian Mixture Probability Hypothesis Density (GM-PHD) filter. Unlike prior approaches that rely on simplistic or uniform assumptions, the proposed Semantic-Occlusion Aware (S-OA) birth model defines initialization terms by explicitly considering regions of occlusion and by leveraging semantic information about the environment. This enables the filter to accurately represent where new objects are more likely to appear, thereby improving tracking performance in complex and high-density driving scenarios. The method is evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations and experiments on the KITTI dataset. Performance is assessed by measuring the latency between first detection and track initiation, along with the mean absolute cardinality error and the Optimal Subpattern Assignment (OSPA) metric. Results demonstrate that the S-OA birth model reduces initialization delay in occlusion-heavy settings, matching or outperforming the strongest baseline in approximately 70% of cases. A sensitivity analysis of birth model weights is also provided. Overall, the findings underscore the benefits of integrating occlusion reasoning and semantic priors into Bayesian tracking frameworks for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [13] [GaussianDream: A Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20752)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Qian Cheng,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianDream提出一种前馈式3D高斯世界模型插件，通过在训练时耦合当前高斯重建与未来高斯预测，为机器人轨迹提供结构化时空监督（RGB、深度、伪3D场景流），推理时仅保留学习到的前缀条件化动作生成，无需测试时解码或规划，在LIBERO、RoboCasa Human-50和真实机器人任务上取得强竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的动作模仿训练对3D几何、密集视觉结构和短视距环境演化提供的显式监督有限，而这些对于物理精确的操作至关重要。

Method: 提出GaussianDream，作为前馈式3D高斯世界模型插件：训练时将当前高斯重建与水平条件化的未来高斯预测耦合，迫使紧凑的时空前缀可解码为可渲染的3D高斯状态，提供密集RGB、深度和伪3D场景流监督；推理时丢弃辅助解码头，仅保留学习到的前缀条件化动作生成，无需渲染、视频推演或额外规划。

Result: LIBERO上平均成功率98.4%，RoboCasa Human-50上52.6%，真实机器人评估中50.0%，均表现出强竞争力。

Conclusion: GaussianDream通过引入3D高斯世界模型的时空监督有效提升了VLA策略的物理精确操作能力，同时保持推理时的高效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation by transferring semantic priors from pretrained vision-language models to action generation. Yet, standard action-imitation training often provides limited explicit supervision for 3D geometry, dense visual structure, and short-horizon environment evolution, which are critical for physically precise manipulation. We introduce \textbf{GaussianDream}, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian world-model plug-in that turns robot trajectories into structured spatial-temporal supervision. The key idea is to couple current Gaussian reconstruction with horizon-conditioned future Gaussian prediction during training, forcing a compact spatio-temporal prefix to be decodable into renderable 3D Gaussian states. This enables dense RGB rendering, depth, and pseudo 3D scene-flow supervision without requiring test-time Gaussian decoding. At inference, GaussianDream discards all auxiliary decoding heads and retains only the learned prefix to condition action generation, avoiding rendering, video rollout, or additional planning during closed-loop control. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa Human-50, and real-robot tasks demonstrate strong and highly competitive performance, achieving \textbf{98.4\%} average success on LIBERO, \textbf{52.6\%} on RoboCasa Human-50, and \textbf{50.0\%} in real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [14] [VLA-REPLICA: A Low-Cost, Reproducible Benchmark for Real-World Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20774)
*Alex S. Huang,Jiahui Zhang,Shiqing Tang,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-REPLICA是一个低成本、易复现的基准测试平台，用于评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在真实世界机器人操作任务中的表现，弥补了现有仿真和真实世界基准测试的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真基准测试无法捕捉真实世界的复杂性，而现有真实世界基准测试则受限于昂贵硬件、集中化评估或任务多样性不足，缺乏可访问、可复现且一致的评估标准。

Method: 使用现成组件构建低成本的真实世界基准测试平台，包含多样化操作任务套件、小规模目标域适应演示数据集，以及面向分布内和分布外场景的评估协议。

Result: 通过与模仿学习和先进VLA模型的实验，揭示了模型在不同任务上的优势与局限性，并在独立构建的实验平台上获得了一致的结果，验证了基准的可复现性。

Conclusion: VLA-REPLICA为VLA模型提供了一种可访问、易复现且一致的评估方案，有助于推动通用机器人操作领域的基准标准化和研究进展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but their real-world evaluation remains limited by a lack of accessible, reproducible, and consistent benchmarks. Simulation benchmarks fail to capture real-world complexity, while existing real-world benchmarks often require expensive hardware, centralized evaluation, or are limited in task diversity. We introduce VLA-REPLICA, a low-cost, easily reproducible real-world benchmark for evaluating VLA models. Built from off-the-shelf components, our system can be quickly assembled and replicated across laboratories, providing a consistent environment for policy evaluation anywhere in the world. VLA-REPLICA includes a diverse suite of manipulation tasks and a small-scale demonstration dataset for target-domain adaptation, with real-world evaluation protocols for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Experiments with imitation learning and state-of-the-art VLA models reveal model strengths and limitations, while consistent results across independently constructed setups demonstrate the reproducibility of our benchmark.

</details>


### [15] [CMC-Opt: Constraint Manifold with Corners for Inequality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20796)
*Yetong Zhang,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流形的框架，通过将带约束的优化问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题，来处理机器人学中的等式和不等式约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学中的优化问题常涉及混合非线性等式与不等式约束，传统方法难以有效处理，尤其是大规模运动规划问题中标准方法经常失败。

Method: 引入“带角的约束流形”来表示满足混合非线性等式与不等式约束的状态空间，并将流形优化算法扩展到该新型拓扑结构上，将原问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题。

Result: 在大规模运动规划问题中成功生成动态可行的轨迹，而标准方法在此任务上失败。

Conclusion: 该流形框架能够鲁棒地处理机器人学中的大规模约束优化问题，在标准方法失效的场景下仍能成功求解。

Abstract: We introduce a manifold-based framework for addressing optimization problems with equality and inequality constraints found in robotics. Our approach transforms the original problem into an unconstrained optimization problem directly on the constrained state space. To achieve this, we introduce ``constraint manifolds with corners" to represent the state space satisfying mixed nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. We further extend manifold optimization algorithms to operate on this new topological structure. We demonstrate the power and robustness of our framework in the context of a large-scale kinodynamic planning problem, successfully generating dynamically feasible trajectories where standard methods fail.

</details>


### [16] [Demo-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for One-shot Cross-Embodiment Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20811)
*Jingyang He,Guangrun Li,Jieyu Zhang,Chengkai Hou,Zhengping Che,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Demo-JEPA，一种基于JEPA世界模型的跨具身模仿学习框架，通过将演示视为隐式目标规范而非具体动作序列，在共享预测表示空间中解耦演示意图与具身执行，实现异构机器人间的灵活模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习将演示视为复制动作序列，但动作天生与具身绑定。当演示来自不同形态、运动学或动作空间的机器人或人类时，需要共享动作空间、启发式重定向或大规模多具身联合训练，这限制了跨具身模仿的泛化能力。

Method: 基于JEPA世界模型，将源端视觉演示转化为目标端可兼容的未来潜在轨迹（共享预测表示空间），目标智能体将这些潜在轨迹作为子目标，通过自身学习的前向动力学模型进行规划来实现。无需动作级对应，仅需视觉演示和目标智能体的自身交互经验。

Result: 在RLBench和真实世界操控任务上，Demo-JEPA达到了与专用领域内规划器相当的性能，并能够在先前方法失败的未见任务和具身配置上泛化。

Conclusion: 通过将演示意图与具身执行解耦，Demo-JEPA展示了跨异构具身模仿学习的可行性，避免了动作级对应和大量多具身数据的需求。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning is often treated as reproducing demonstrated actions, but actions are inherently embodiment-specific. When demonstrations come from humans or robots with different morphology, kinematics, or action spaces, this action-centric view requires shared action spaces, heuristic retargeting, or large-scale multi-embodiment co-training. We instead view demonstrations as implicit specifications of future goals: the target agent should infer what state the demonstrator is trying to realize, rather than how the demonstrator executes it. We propose Demo-JEPA, a cross-embodiment imitation framework that decouples demonstration intent from embodiment-specific execution. Built on a JEPA-based world model, Demo-JEPA translates source visual demonstrations into target-compatible future latent trajectories in a shared predictive representation space. The target agent then uses these latent trajectories as subgoals and realizes them through planning under its own learned forward dynamics. Because Demo-JEPA avoids action-level correspondence and requires only visual demonstrations plus the target agent's own interaction experience, it supports flexible imitation across heterogeneous embodiments. Experiments on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks show that Demo-JEPA matches specialized in-domain planners and generalizes to unseen tasks and embodiment configurations where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [17] [Mobile UMI: Cross-View Diffusion Policy with Decoupled Kinematics for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20894)
*Haoran Huang,Haonan Dong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mobile UMI，一种无需硬件的移动模仿学习框架，通过双摄像头采集系统、基于ChArUco的空间锚定解耦操作与基座轨迹，以及异步滚动时域执行器进行在线状态匹配，在四个长时域家庭任务中达到83.8%的平均成功率，有效解决了移动基座上的动作标签污染和推理延迟两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动模仿学习面临两个耦合瓶颈：1） locomotion-contaminated action labels（移动基座运动污染的动作标签）；2） inference-induced execution latency on a continuously moving base（连续移动基座上推理引起的执行延迟）。现有腕装界面仅适用于桌面数据采集，无法捕获基座导航所需的全局上下文；体装相机则会将人类行走与手部运动纠缠；同时生成式策略引入数百毫秒推理延迟，导致基座越过预测路径点，需要向后修正。

Method: 提出Mobile UMI框架，包含三个组件：1）双摄像头采集系统，记录胸部全局上下文和腕部局部交互，无需机器人参与；2）基于ChArUco的单次空间锚定，统一胸部与手部视觉-惯性坐标系，将手部姿态相对于胸部重新表达，提取解耦的SE(3)操作轨迹和SE(2)基座轨迹；3）异步滚动时域执行器，进行在线状态匹配，将每个生成的动作块与当前物理姿态重新对齐，丢弃过期路径点后再执行。

Result: 在四个长时域家庭任务上评估，每个任务100次试验，平均成功率83.8%。对比实验表明，仅胸部相对动作标签就大幅缩小了差距，在线状态匹配弥补了剩余差距。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，显式的运动学分解结合状态级延迟对齐为移动模仿学习提供了有效解决方案，无需修改底层策略类的架构。

Abstract: Mobile imitation learning on portable demonstration interfaces faces two coupled bottlenecks: locomotion-contaminated action labels and inference-induced execution latency on a continuously moving base. Recent wrist-mounted interfaces lower the cost of tabletop data collection, yet a single wrist view does not capture the global context required for base navigation. Adding a body-mounted camera entangles human walking with hand motion. Meanwhile, generative policies introduce hundreds of milliseconds of inference latency, during which the base advances past predicted waypoints, forcing backward corrections at action splices. This paper presents Mobile UMI, a hardware-free demonstration framework that addresses both gaps through three components. First, a dual-camera capture system records chest-centric global context and wrist-centric local interaction without any robot present. Second, a one-shot ChArUco-based spatial anchor unifies the chest and hand visual-inertial frames; the hand pose is then re-expressed relative to the chest to extract decoupled SE(3) manipulation and SE(2) base trajectories. Third, an asynchronous receding-horizon executor performs online state matching: each generated action chunk is realigned with the current physical pose so that expired waypoints are discarded before execution. The full system is evaluated on four long-horizon household tasks, achieving an average success rate of 83.8% over 100 trials per task. Controlled comparisons against ACT and Diffusion Policy show that the chest-relative label alone closes much of the gap; online state matching closes the remainder. These results indicate that, for mobile imitation learning under the tested conditions, explicit kinematic factorization combined with state-level latency alignment provides an effective solution without requiring architectural changes to the underlying policy class.

</details>


### [18] [SubTGraph: Large-Scale Subterranean Environment Synthesis with Controllable Topological Variability for Robotic Autonomy Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20917)
*F. Labra Caso,A. Saradagi,S. Fredriksson,S. Nordström,A. Koval,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SubTGraph框架，能够快速合成多层级、高变异性的地下环境，用于自主机器人系统的严格统计验证，并开源了150个地下世界数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境是自主机器人的前沿领域，但真实环境难以获取，现有研究缺乏大规模仿真基准基础设施来进行严格的统计评估。

Method: SubTGraph利用用户指定的结构约束构建成本矩阵，引导经典Dijkstra算法，结合DARPA World Generator的拓扑度量瓦片，程序化生成多样化的地下世界。

Result: 通过三个机器人案例研究验证了框架的有效性：结构语义分割验证、多智能体路径规划模式识别、LIO SLAM压力测试识别失败情况。开源了代码库和150个高变异地下世界数据库。

Conclusion: SubTGraph填补了地下环境大规模仿真基准评估的空白，为机器人自主栈各层提供了严格的统计验证工具。

Abstract: Subterranean (SubT) environments have been a frontier for autonomous robotics, driven by the push for automation of mining operations and the interest in planetary exploration (Martian Lava Tubes). Due to the challenges involved in accessing real SubT environments, rigorous hardening of autonomy stacks in realistic simulation environments is critical. This article fills a well-known gap, which relates to the unavailability of a large-scale simulation-based benchmarking infrastructure for rigorous statistical evaluation of robotic autonomy, due to which it is common for SubT research articles to present validation results in a few environments at best.
  This article presents SubTGraph, a novel framework for rapid synthesis of multi-level SubT environments with high variability, incorporating user specifications related to topology, dimensionality, textures, etc., to generate distinct environments such as operational mines, natural caves and lava tubes. SubTGraph builds a cost matrix from user-specified structural constraints to guide the classical Dijkstra algorithm to procedurally generate SubT worlds utilizing topometric tiles from the DARPA World Generator. Three robotics case-studies are investigated to demonstrate the utility of SubTGraph for rigorous validation of different layers in the robotic autonomy stack. Structural semantic segmentation is validated against topometric ground truths, multi-agent path planning is widely tested for identification of patterns and trends in the algorithm behavior and LIO SLAM is stress-tested in challenging subterranean sections to identify failure cases. The SubTGraph world creation codebase is open-sourced (https://github.com/LTU-RAI/SubTGraph.git) along with a database consisting of 150 highly variable underground worlds.

</details>


### [19] [STEAM: A Training-Free Congestion-Aware Enhancement Framework for Decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20929)
*Mingyang Feng,Mengnuo Zhang,Shaoyuan Li,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEAM是一种无需训练、仅在测试时增强的框架，通过注入轻量级拥塞感知引导来提升基于学习的分散式多智能体路径规划（MAPF）性能，无需重新训练或修改架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的分散式MAPF方法在密集场景下易产生拥塞和冲突，而集中式方法可扩展性差。作者希望在不增加训练成本、不改动原有策略架构的前提下，提升分散式策略的拥塞处理能力。

Method: 1) 通过最短路径展开识别未来拥塞热点；2) 空间可避让拥塞：更新智能体专属cost-to-go地图；3) 空间不可避让瓶颈：通过时序logit修正处理；4) 涌现局部拥塞：基于相邻智能体修正后的cost-to-go地图进行密度感知的logit修正。

Result: 在多个代表性基于学习的分散式MAPF算法上的实验表明，STEAM持续提升了成功率、完工时间和解代价，成功率提升高达60%，且仅带来极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: STEAM作为一种轻量级、即插即用的测试时增强框架，能有效提升分散式MAPF算法的拥塞感知能力，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose STEAM (Spatial, Temporal, and Emergent congestion Awareness for MAPF), a training-free test-time enhancement framework for learning-based decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) in discrete environments. Given a pretrained decentralized policy, STEAM requires no retraining, architectural modification, or replacement by a centralized planner. Instead, it injects lightweight congestion-aware guidance into the original policy execution. STEAM first rolls out the shortest paths induced by the current cost-to-go maps to identify potential future congestion hotspots. Spatially avoidable congestion is mitigated by updating agent-specific cost-to-go information, while spatially unavoidable bottlenecks are handled through temporal logit correction. In addition, emergent local congestion is reduced by a density-aware logit correction based on neighboring agents' corrected cost-to-go maps. Extensive experiments on representative learning-based decentralized MAPF algorithms show that STEAM consistently improves success rate, makespan, and solution cost, with success-rate gains of up to 60% and only minor computational overhead. The implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/STEAM-MAPF-7A62.

</details>


### [20] [WiXus: A Wheeled-Legged Robot with Wire-Driven Environmental Utilizing to Integrate Mobility and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20932)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并开发了WiXus机器人，将轮腿机构与线驱机构融合，通过外部环境支撑使腿部从 locomotion 中解放，实现物体操作和工具使用，拓展了轮腿机器人的操作域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人仅作为专用于 locomotion 的平台，腿部无法兼顾操作任务（如物体操纵或工具使用），缺乏利用环境支撑来释放腿部潜能的机制。

Method: 提出 WiXus 机器人，融合轮腿机构与利用外部环境的线驱机构；通过线驱悬挂身体使腿部从 locomotion 中解放，重新用作手臂进行物体操纵和工具使用。

Result: WiXus 实现平面轮腿 locomotion、线驱与轮腿协调驱动下的三维运动（如攀爬悬崖），以及腿部被解放后执行物体操纵（救援狗玩偶）和工具使用（用修枝剪采摘苹果模型）等任务。

Conclusion: 利用线驱机构来利用环境支撑是一种新的设计原则，可有效拓展轮腿机器人的操作域。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots, which have wheels at their feet and achieve high mobility by coordinating wheel drive and leg drive, have been developed. These robots have been developed purely as platforms specialized for locomotion. Therefore, they do not have a means to repurpose their legs for roles other than locomotion, such as object manipulation or tool utilization. In this paper, we address the problem of how to draw out the potential task-execution capability of the legs by freeing them from the roles of locomotion through external body support. To this end, we propose and develop a new robot, WiXus, which fuses a wheeled-legged mechanism with a wire-driven mechanism that utilizes the external environment. The developed WiXus demonstrates not only planar locomotion with wheeled-legged drive, but also three-dimensional mobility such as cliff climbing by coordinating wire-driven and wheeled-legged actuation. Furthermore, by suspending the body with wire-driven actuation, WiXus successfully repurpose its legs as arms to perform object manipulation, (e.g., rescuing a dog (stuffed animal)), and tool utilization (e.g., harvesting an apple (mockup) with loppers). This study demonstrates that the approach of utilizing the environment with wire-driven actuation is a new design principle that extends the operational domain of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [21] [Component Influence-Driven Fastener Reduction for Robotic Disassemblability-Aware Design Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21026)
*Takuya Kiyokawa,Tomoki Ishikura,Shingo Hamada,Genichiro Matsuda,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析框架，通过CAD模型和CCC图将机器人拆卸序列规划结果转化为组件影响分数，并以3D热图形式可视化，帮助设计师识别和移除冗余紧固件，从而简化结构依赖、减少工具更换和缩短机器人移动距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化再制造过程中，产品设计阶段需要考虑机器人拆卸，但设计师缺乏量化反馈来识别哪些结构元件阻碍了机器人操作，因此需要一种能够提供可操作重新设计指导的分析框架。

Method: 基于CAD模型及其自动生成的接触-连接-约束（CCC）图，将机器人拆卸序列规划结果转化为组件影响分数，投影到CAD几何上生成3D热图；再通过分析模拟移除高影响紧固件，评估结构约束、工具更换和机器人移动距离的减少，同时利用几何稳定性指标防止不安全修改。

Result: 在七种家用电器上的实验表明，移除推荐的紧固件后，每种产品的结构依赖图减少了8至132个结构约束；消除了不必要的工具更换操作，并在结构允许的条件下将机器人移动距离缩短了165至1675毫米。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效识别冗余紧固件，为设计师提供量化反馈以改进产品设计，提升机器人拆卸效率和自动化再制造的可行性。

Abstract: To accelerate automated remanufacturing, robotic disassembly must be considered during the product design phase. However, designers currently lack quantitative feedback to identify which structural elements hinder robotic operations. To address this, this study proposes an analytical framework that provides actionable redesign guidance focused on fastener reduction, as fasteners are numerous and ubiquitous components found in almost all manufactured products. Using a Computer-Aided Design (CAD) model and its automatically generated Contact-Connection-Constraint (CCC) graph, the framework translates robotic disassembly sequence planning outcomes into component influence scores. These scores reflect how often a component causes structural constraint violations or evaluation objective deteriorations in the robotic disassembly sequence. To visually highlight structural hindrances, the framework projects these scores onto the CAD geometry as 3D heatmaps. The system then analytically simulates the removal of highly influential fasteners. It reports the expected reductions in structural constraints, tool changes, and robot travel distances, while preventing structurally unsafe modifications by evaluating geometric stability metrics. Experiments on seven household appliances demonstrate that the framework successfully targets redundant fasteners. Removing the recommended fasteners simplified the structural dependencies by eliminating between 8 and 132 structural constraints on the graph depending on each product's structural configuration. Furthermore, it improved robotic operational efficiency by eliminating unnecessary tool change operations and shortening travel distances by 165 to 1675 millimeters wherever structurally permissible.

</details>


### [22] [Modeling and Control of a Pneumatic Morphing Soft Quadrotor based on the SOFA Framework for Dynamic Soft Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21031)
*F. Labra Caso,V. Sumathy,P. Ferrentino,V. Vanderborght,J. Haluska,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SOFA的有限元方法，用于对气动变形软体四旋翼飞行器进行软体建模、动态仿真与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼动力学无法捕捉气动软体臂复杂的时变行为，需要一种既能保持物理可解释性和控制结构，又能准确建模软体变形的方法。

Method: 采用SOFA框架，将气动软体臂离散化为四面体网格，基于弹性材料法则生成与实际动态行为匹配的内力；通过周期性和误差基控制信号驱动内部空腔实现气动变形；设计比例-积分控制器实现目标位置控制。

Result: 仿真结果证明了所提建模框架及控制器设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效保留了传统四旋翼动力学的控制结构，同时能够准确捕捉软体臂的复杂变形行为，为软体变形四旋翼的建模与控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This article presents a novel SOFA based finite element method for the soft body modeling and the corresponding dynamic simulation and control of a pneumatic morphing soft quadrotor. The proposed modeling preserves the physical interpretability and control structure of traditional quadrotor dynamics, while capturing the complex, time-varying behavior of pneumatically actuated soft arms. In SOFA, the soft pneumatically actuated arms are discretized as a tetrahedral mesh following an elastic material law that produces internal forces adequate to the real dynamic behavior of the body. Pneumatic actuation governed by both periodic and error-based control signals is applied within the internal cavities to analyze the morphing capability. Finally, a proportional-integral controller is proposed to study the controlled dynamic behavior and morphing capabilities of the pneumatic arm, wherein the pneumatic actuation to the soft arm is controlled to achieve the desired target position. The simulation results show the effectiveness of the proposed novel modeling framework and the related controller design.

</details>

*Hana Yamamoto,Carlotta Julia Mayer,Charlotte Raithel,Theresa Buchner,Christian Werner,Yasuhisa Hirata,Monika Eckstein,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过多模态分析（面部表情、心率变异性、主观问卷）比较了老年人与社交机器人和人类互动时的心理生理反应，发现机器人可被接受为有效互动伙伴且不增加心理压力，甚至带来更放松的状态，有望缓解照护者负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球照护人员短缺问题，需要深入理解社交辅助机器人在人机交互中对老年人的心理和生理影响，探究社交机器人能否成为有效的互动伙伴。

Method: 对35名70岁以上老年人进行对比实验，采用多模态分析方法，整合面部表情数据、心率变异性（HRV）和主观问卷进行评估。

Result: 人机互动与人人互动在整体压力水平上无显著差异；面部表情分析证实机器人被接受为有效互动伙伴；生理数据显示机器人互动时心率略低，表明更放松的状态。

Conclusion: 社交机器人能够在不引起心理压力的情况下与老年人互动，可通过执行结构化任务（如健康感知调查）减轻照护者负担；未来需解决设计中"外观-内容不匹配"问题以促进更自然有效的交互。

Abstract: Addressing the global caregiver shortage through socially assistive robots necessitates a deep understanding of their psychological and physiological impacts on older adults during human-robot interaction (HRI). This study addresses whether social robots can serve as effective interaction partners compared to humans, and if "positive prompts" can similarly enhance these interactions. We conducted a comparative study with 35 participants (aged 70+). Our multi-modal analysis, integrating facial expression data, heart rate variability, and subjective questionnaires, revealed no significant differences in overall stress levels between human and robot interactions. Facial expression analysis confirmed that the robot was accepted as a valid interaction partner, while physiological data showed slightly lower heart rates during robot interactions, suggesting a more relaxed state compared to human-led sessions. These findings indicate that social robots can engage older adults without inducing psychological strain and are capable of alleviating caregiver burden by performing structured tasks, such as health-sensing surveys. Future work should address the identified "appearance-content mismatch" in robot design to facilitate even more natural and effective interactions.

</details>


### [24] [Anomaly-Informed Confidence Calibration for Vision-Based Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21109)
*Zhenjiang Mao,Jiawen Wu,Gabriel Wagner,Zhongzheng Zhang,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异常感知的在线校准方法，融合世界模型中的感知异常分数（重建误差）和动态异常分数（认知不确定性和控制流统计），无需重新训练即可有效校准视觉控制器在分布偏移下的过度自信问题，在真实DonkeyCar平台的四种异常协议下将期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车中基于视觉的控制器在测试时遭遇分布偏移会变得过度自信，现有异常信号（如自编码器重建误差）只能捕获视觉损坏，却遗漏了动态异常（如执行器偏差、延迟），即图像看似正常但轨迹已退化，存在感知-动力学鸿沟。

Method: 提出Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration方法，无需重新训练任何模型组件，融合来自世界模型的两个互补异常分数：感知分数（重建误差）和动态分数（认知不确定性与控制流统计）；基于融合分数，使用轻量级温度缩放校准器，结合测试时增强技术在分布偏移下选择性降低过度自信，同时保持名义条件性能。

Result: 在物理DonkeyCar平台上，针对四种训练时未见过的真实异常（黑暗、模糊、执行器偏差、处理延迟），平均期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，相比最佳基线提升37%。

Conclusion: 融合感知与动态异常分数的在线校准方法能有效弥补现有方法对动态异常信号缺失的问题，在不修改基础安全预测器的情况下显著提升分布偏移下的置信度校准质量。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are important for safely deploying vision-based controllers in autonomous racing, where safety predictions must be derived from camera images, yet modern predictors become dangerously overconfident under test-time distribution shifts. We identify a critical perception-dynamics gap in existing anomaly signals: widely used scores, such as autoencoder reconstruction error, capture visual corruptions but miss dynamics anomalies (e.g., actuation bias, latency), where images remain plausible while the trajectory degrades. To address this, we propose an Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration approach that, without retraining any model component, fuses two complementary anomaly scores extracted from a world model: a perceptual score from reconstruction error and a dynamics score from epistemic uncertainty and control-stream statistics. Based on these fused scores, a lightweight temperature-scaling calibrator leverages test-time augmentation to selectively reduce overconfidence under shift while preserving nominal-condition performance. Experiments on a physical DonkeyCar under four real-world anomaly protocols unseen during training (darkness, blur, actuation bias, processing latency) reduce average expected calibration error from 0.184 to 0.116, a 37% improvement over the best baseline, without modifying the base safety predictor.

</details>


### [25] [Benchmarking Empirical and Learning-Based Approaches for Feedforward Steering Control in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21111)
*Georg Jank,Mattia Piccinini,Sebastian Wenk,Phillip Pitschi,Johannes Betz,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了两种学习型（基于学习）和两种经验型（解析）的自主赛车前馈转向控制器，提出了一种基于多项式曲面拟合的新型EHD方法。开环评估中学习型控制器预测误差最低，但闭环测试中其精度优势并未转化为更优的路径跟踪性能或更快的圈速；而所提出的EHD方法在闭环鲁棒性和圈速上表现最佳，强调了在前馈策略评估中必须考虑完整的轨迹规划与控制软件栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少反馈控制器的转向修正量需要准确预测车辆的逆侧向动力学，现有前馈转向控制方法缺乏系统性基准对比，且需要一种既能捕捉速度相关非线性转向行为又具有最小参数化的方法。

Method: 提出了基于多项式曲面拟合的新型扩展航向差（EHD）公式；在基于阿布扎比自主赛车联盟真实赛道的高保真双轨车辆动力学仿真框架中，对两种学习型（基于学习）和两种经验型（解析）前馈转向控制器进行了开环和闭环系统性基准测试。

Result: 开环评估中学习型控制器预测误差最低；但闭环测试中其精度优势并未转化为更优的轨迹跟踪性能或更快的圈速；所提EHD方法在闭环鲁棒性和圈速上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 前馈策略的评估必须在完整的轨迹规划与控制软件栈中进行，单纯的开环预测精度不能保证闭环性能。

Abstract: Feedforward steering control is a key component of hierarchical control architectures for autonomous racing. The goal is to reduce steering corrections from the feedback controllers by predicting the vehicle's inverse lateral dynamics. This paper presents a systematic benchmark of two learning-based and two empirical (analytical) feedforward steering controllers. We introduce a new \acf{ehd} formulation based on a polynomial surface fit that captures velocity-dependent nonlinear steering behavior with minimal parametrization. We test the feedforward controllers in a high-fidelity simulation framework based on the real-world Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League competition, using a high-fidelity double-track vehicle dynamics simulator. Open-loop evaluation shows that the learning-based controllers achieve the lowest prediction errors; however, closed-loop testing reveals that this improved accuracy does not translate into superior path tracking performance or lap times, even after iterative fine-tuning. In contrast, the proposed EHD approach achieves the best overall closed-loop robustness and lap time, highlighting the necessity of evaluating feedforward strategies within the complete trajectory planning and control software stack. Our code is available at https://github.com/TUMRT/steering_ff_control.

</details>


### [26] [Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Active Spatial Brain and Generalizable Action Cerebellum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21133)
*Zhizhao Liang,Yi-Lin Wei,Xuhang Chen,Mu Lin,Yi-Xiang He,Zhexi Luo,Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体大模型的空间感知人形全身操控框架（Active Spatial Brain + Generalizable Action Cerebellum），无需任务特定真实机器人数据即可实现复杂3D环境中的泛化操作，在多种空间操控任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有桌面级操控方法难以应对复杂3D环境中的空间理解挑战，且真实机器人数据获取成本高、泛化能力受限，亟需一种无需大量真实数据即可泛化的人形机器人全身操控框架。

Method: 提出双组件框架：1) Active Spatial Brain（主动空间大脑）负责主动空间感知、任务规划与子任务分解；2) Generalizable Action Cerebellum（泛化动作小脑）根据决策生成可执行机器人动作，无需任务特定的真实机器人数据。两组件协同实现从空间理解到动作执行的完整流程。

Result: 设计了从空间感知理解和真实机器人任务性能两个维度的基准测试，在多种任务和环境中均展现出强性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体大模型框架有效解决了人形全身操控中的空间理解和动作泛化两大挑战，无需任务特定真实数据即可实现泛化，为复杂3D环境中的人形机器人操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore spatial-aware humanoid whole-body manipulation task. Compared with tabletop settings, this task poses two key challenges: 1) Spatial understanding is challenging in complex 3D environments with diverse spatial relations. 2) Action generation is difficult to generalize, as limited and costly real-robot data restricts data-driven models generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation framework that leverages the spatial perception and action generation capabilities of multi-agent large models. Specifically, our framework includes two components: Active Spatial Brain for active spatial perception and decision-making, and Generalizable Action Cerebellum for executable robot action generation. The first component actively perceives the spatial scene and makes decisions on task planning and subtask decomposition. The second component generate executable robot actions based on the decisions made by the first module without needs of task-specific real robot data. To benchmark our framework, we design a set of spatial manipulation tasks from two perspectives: evaluating spatial perception and understanding, and assessing real-robot task performance. The results demonstrate strong performance on both aspects across diverse tasks and environments.

</details>


### [27] [Safety-Critical Control for Smoothed Implicit Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21138)
*Haegu Lee,Yitaek Kim,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对平滑隐式接触动力学中的安全关键控制问题，提出了一种通过边界聚焦轨迹筛选和鲁棒控制屏障函数（CBF）来约束接触力、消除力违规的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑隐式接触动力学使得基于梯度的规划和控制成为可能，但安全关键控制仍具挑战，因为隐式接触动力学使得安全滤波器设计困难，且平滑参数κ会松弛接触互补约束，影响接触力精度。

Method: 1. 证明约束违反与κ呈非单调关系；2. 提出边界聚焦轨迹筛选（boundary-focused rollouts）方法，通过比较安全裕度与近似误差来筛选κ；3. 基于隐式接触力的一阶泰勒近似，构建离散时间控制屏障函数（CBF）框架；4. 引入固定鲁棒裕度以应对力低估问题。

Result: 在四个接触丰富系统的仿真中，所提出的方法消除了标准CBF下观测到的力违规现象。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在平滑隐式接触动力学中有效约束实际接触力，提升安全关键控制性能，即使采用松弛的互补约束也能保证安全性。

Abstract: Smoothed implicit contact dynamics enables gradient-based planning and control for contact-rich tasks without predefined mode sequences. However, safety-critical control remains challenging because implicit contact dynamics makes safety-filter design nontrivial. The smoothing parameter $κ$ relaxes contact complementarity constraints, which makes the dynamics smooth but affects the contact force. This paper provides a method for bounding the actual contact force despite the use of relaxed complementarity constraints. We show that constraint violations can be non-monotonic in $κ$. Smaller $κ$ reduces force-approximation error, but it does not necessarily improve safety performance. To address this issue, we introduce boundary-focused rollouts to screen $κ$ by comparing the safety margin with the approximation error. We then develop a discrete-time control barrier function (CBF) framework based on a first-order Taylor approximation of the implicitly defined contact force. To account for possible force under-prediction, we augment the resulting safety constraint with a fixed robust margin. Simulations on four contact-rich systems show that the proposed method eliminates force violations observed under a standard CBF.

</details>


### [28] [EllipseLIO: Adaptive LiDAR Inertial Odometry with an Ellipsoid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21150)
*Rowan Border,Margarita Chli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EllipseLIO是一种无需场景特定调参即可在不同环境和LiDAR传感器下自适应工作的实时LIO方法，在五个多样化数据集上平均误差比第二名低38%，且是唯一在所有实验中均未发散的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO方法在特定场景下调参后表现可靠，但在异构环境和传感器下使用统一配置时难以保持鲁棒性，亟需一种无需人工干预即可自适应不同场景的LIO方法。

Method: 提出EllipseLIO方法，采用自适应LiDAR扫描滤波与配准技术，根据传感器能力和环境特征动态调整，无需针对特定场景进行调参。

Result: 在五个多样化且具有挑战性的数据集上，EllipseLIO总体表现最佳，平均里程计误差比第二名低38%，且是唯一在所有实验中均未发散的方法。

Conclusion: EllipseLIO通过自适应滤波与配准策略，有效提升了跨异构环境和传感器的里程计泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Abstract: LiDAR Inertial Odometry (LIO) is a critical component for many mobile robots that need to navigate without relying on external positioning (e.g., GPS). Platforms that operate autonomously in different environments and with heterogeneous LiDAR sensors require a LIO approach that can adapt to these different scenarios without human intervention.
  Existing LIO approaches can typically provide reliable and accurate odometry in scenarios with similar environments and sensors when suitably tuned. However, many approaches struggle to retain robust odometry across heterogeneous environments and sensors while using a consistent configuration.
  This paper presents EllipseLIO, a real-time LIO approach that generalises between scenarios by using methods for LiDAR scan filtering and registration that adapt to the sensor capabilities and environment without requiring scenario-specific tuning. Experiments with EllipseLIO and state-of-the-art LIO approaches on five datasets with diverse and challenging scenarios demonstrate that EllipseLIO is the best-performing approach overall. It achieves a 38% lower odometry error on average than the second-best approach and is the only approach that does not diverge in any experiment. An open-source version of EllipseLIO will be available at github.com/v4rl-ucy/ellipselio.

</details>


### [29] [A Terrain-Adaptive epsilon-Constraint MPC for Uneven Terrain Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21188)
*Otobong Jerome,Geesara Kalathunga,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应epsilon约束MPC方法，结合半参数模型（解析动力学+稀疏高斯过程），在崎岖地形上实现车辆动力学规划，同时优化路径效率和位姿稳定性等竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在崎岖地形上，类车机器人的动力学规划需要同时优化路径效率和位姿稳定性等相互竞争的目标，现有方法难以在实时性下有效探索帕累托前沿。

Method: 采用自适应epsilon约束方法集成到MPC框架中，根据地形描述符动态调整epsilon边界以实时探索帕累托前沿；构建半参数模型（解析车辆动力学+基于相同地形描述符训练的稀疏高斯过程）来捕捉车辆-地形动力学。

Result: 与MPPI和GAKD基线相比，导航成功率达94%，最大方向偏差降低24%，多目标权衡质量提升23%。

Conclusion: 所提epsilon-MPC方法在崎岖地形动力学规划中有效平衡了多个竞争目标，显著提升了导航性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Kinodynamic planning for car-like vehicles on uneven terrain requires simultaneously optimizing competing objectives such as path efficiency and pose stability. This work presents an adaptive epsilon-constraint method integrated into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, where the epsilon bounds are dynamically adjusted based on terrain descriptors to explore the Pareto front in real time. To capture vehicle-terrain dynamics, we develop a semi-parametric model combining analytical vehicle dynamics with a Sparse Gaussian Process (SGP) trained on the same terrain descriptors. The proposed epsilon-MPC is evaluated against MPPI and GAKD baselines, achieving a 94% navigation success rate while reducing maximum orientation deviation by 24% and improving multi-objective trade-off quality by 23%.

</details>


### [30] [To Select or not to Select, that is the Question: Distilling Robot Skill Prediction into a Small Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21242)
*Haechan Mark Bong,Simon Roy,Euhid Aman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对异构机器人集群的任务分配问题，本文通过LLM辅助生成与标注审核构建合成任务-技能数据集，训练一个约1.33亿参数的双句编码器集成模型（mpnet+MiniLM），在任务-技能匹配任务上达到83.5%的准确率，显著优于Kimi K2（72.0%）、GPT-OSS-120B（71.5%）等大型通用LLM，表明针对固定机器人技能分类的小型专用模型可超越大型通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群日益异构化（包括人形机器人、轮式机器人、四足机器人、无人机等），如何为给定任务选择最合适的机器人成为一个核心系统问题。现有方法缺乏将自然语言任务描述映射到机器人物理能力（如飞行、轮式、腿式、水面/水下作业、手部操作等）的标注数据。

Method: 利用LLM辅助生成和针对性标签审核构建合成任务-技能数据集；训练一个约1.33亿参数的集成模型（两个微调句编码器mpnet + MiniLM的集成）；在分层抽样的200任务数据集上进行评估。

Result: 集成模型在任务-技能匹配上达到83.5%准确率，优于Kimi K2（1T MoE, 72.0%）、GPT-OSS-120B（71.5%）和Llama-4-Scout-17B（69.0%）在同一零样本提示下的表现。

Conclusion: 对于固定的机器人技能分类体系，基于合成数据训练的小型专用模型在集群级任务路由问题上可以显著超越参数量大得多的通用大语言模型。

Abstract: As robot fleets become more heterogeneous, including humanoids, rovers, quadrupeds, and drones, selecting the right robot for a task becomes a core systems problem. We study robot skill prediction: mapping a natural-language task description to the physical capabilities required to execute it, such as fly, wheels, legs, surface water, under water and hands. Since labelled data that maps natural-language task descriptions to robot's physical capabilities does not exist, we construct a synthetic task-to-skill dataset using LLM-assisted generation and targeted label auditing. Trained on this data, a ~133M-parameter ensemble of two fine-tuned sentence encoders (mpnet + MiniLM) reaches 83.5% task-to-skill matching on a stratified 200 task dataset, outperforming Kimi K2 (1T MoE) at 72.0%, GPT-OSS-120B at 71.5%, and Llama-4-Scout-17B at 69.0% under the same zero-shot prompt. These results suggest that, for fixed robot skill taxonomies, small specialized models trained on synthetic data can outperform much larger general-purpose LLMs for fleet-level task routing.

</details>


### [31] [Reinforcement Learning for Risk Adaptation via Differentiable CVaR Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21257)
*Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端风险自适应框架，结合强化学习与基于CVaR障碍函数的可微二次规划安全层，用于在障碍物运动不确定（高斯混合模型建模）的拥挤环境中进行导航，通过学习名义控制输入、风险等级和安全边界，在安全性和效率间实现上下文感知的自适应平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍物运动不确定的拥挤环境中进行路径规划时，随机交互往往导致过度保守的行为或降低效率，现有方法难以在安全性和效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出端到端风险自适应框架，将强化学习与基于条件风险值（CVaR）障碍函数的可微二次规划安全层相结合，联合学习名义控制输入、风险等级和安全边界，施加显式的概率安全约束。

Result: 在不同障碍物密度和机器人模型下的动态、不确定、拥挤环境中，以及在三种分布外泛化场景下，该方法在安全性、效率和泛化能力上均优于基于优化、基于RL以及RL与优化集成的方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过上下文感知的风险自适应机制，在必要时才触发谨慎行为，在不确定性下实现了安全性、效率和泛化能力的最佳综合性能。

Abstract: Planning through crowded environments under uncertain obstacle motions remains difficult, as stochastic interactions often induce overly conservative behavior or reduced efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end risk adaptation framework for crowd navigation under obstacle-motion uncertainty modeled by a Gaussian mixture model. The framework combines reinforcement learning~(RL) with a differentiable quadratic-program safety layer based on Conditional Value-at-Risk~(CVaR) barrier functions, jointly learning nominal control input, risk level, and safety margin and enforcing explicit probabilistic safety constraints. This design enables context-aware adaptation, promoting efficient behavior while invoking caution only when necessary. We conduct extensive evaluations in dynamic, uncertain, and crowded environments across varying obstacle densities and robot models, and further assess generalization under three out-of-distribution cases. Comparisons across optimization-based, RL-based, and integrated RL and optimization methods are provided, and the proposed method is shown to deliver the strongest overall performance in safety, efficiency, and generalization under uncertainty.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Structural Latent Points for Efficient Visual Representations in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21258)
*Yicheng Jiang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Zesen Gan,Junhao Li,Qiang Zhang,Jingkai Sun,Jiahang Cao,Mingyuan Sun,Xiangyu Yue,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合表征——结构隐式点（structural latent points），通过点云变分自编码器在隐空间中联合正则化点特征和坐标，结合隐式神经场的表达力与显式几何的结构先验，并搭配轻量级3DGS渲染管线，在多个机器人操作基准上显著提升任务成功率、样本效率及鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D感知预训练方法要么使用完全隐式神经场（缺乏结构线索），要么使用完全显式几何基元（分辨率受限、泛化弱），二者难以兼顾表达力与结构性。

Method: 提出结构隐式点（structural latent points）框架：在点云自编码器的隐空间中插入逐点潜变分自编码器，联合正则化点特征与坐标至高斯先验；同时设计轻量级3DGS渲染管线，将更多表征能力留给前端隐模块。

Result: 在RLBench、ManiSkill2和真实机器人平台上，相比强基线方法，在任务成功率、样本效率、视角与场景变化鲁棒性上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 混合表征策略有效融合了隐式表达的丰富性与显式几何的结构先验，各部分设计对整体性能均不可或缺。

Abstract: Current 3D-aware pretraining methods for embodied perception and manipulation are largely built on differentiable rendering frameworks, producing either fully implicit neural fields or fully explicit geometric primitives. Implicit representations, while expressive, lack explicit structural cues, whereas explicit ones preserve geometry but suffer from resolution limits and weak generalization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel pretraining framework that learns a hybrid representation-structural latent points. Specifically, we insert a point-wise latent variational autoencoder into the latent space of a point-cloud autoencoder, jointly regularizing point-wise features and coordinates toward a Gaussian prior. The resulting compact latent preserves coarse structural tendencies, which do not encode precise geometry but capture richer rough shape and semantic information, effectively combining the expressiveness of implicit representations with the structural priors of explicit ones. In addition, informed by shared design choices in prior work, we develop a streamlined, efficient 3DGS-based rendering pipeline that is deliberately kept lightweight, improving efficiency while leaving greater representational capacity to the front-end latent module. Extensive evaluations on RLBench, ManiSkill2, and a real-robot platform demonstrate consistent gains in task success, sample efficiency, and robustness to viewpoint and scene variations over strong baselines. Ablation studies further confirm that each component of our framework is critical to overall performance.

</details>


### [33] [From swept contact to pose: Probe-aware registration via complementary-shape docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21398)
*Chen Chen,Yunwen Li,Yifan Xu,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无标定的接触式配准方法，将接触配准重新定义为物体与探针扫掠体之间的互补形状对接，利用3D FFT相关性和李代数优化实现高精度配准，在牙齿预备机器人上达到0.42 mm和3.75°的精度，优于光学跟踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学配准方法存在标定链条长、视线受限和制造误差等问题，亟需一种无需外部传感器、无标定的替代方案来实现高精度机器人操作中的配准。

Method: 将接触配准建模为物体与探针扫掠体之间的互补形状对接；采用全局到局部的搜索策略，先通过3D FFT相关性和低差异SO(3)采样进行全局搜索，再使用李代数更新和分析接触灵敏度进行连续SE(3)精化。

Result: 在自由形态网格仿真中达到亚0.04 mm和亚0.4°的精度；在牙齿预备机器人上达到0.42 mm和3.75°，优于光学跟踪器。

Conclusion: 该方法为手术和工业机器人提供了一种实用且精确的无外部传感器配准策略，验证了接触配准在实际应用中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Accurate registration between a prior model and the real scene is essential for high-precision robotic manipulation, yet optical methods suffer from long calibration chains, line-of-sight constraints, and fabrication errors. We propose a calibration-free alternative that reformulates contact registration as complementary-shape docking between the object and the probe's swept volume, explicitly accounting for probe geometry and leveraging both contact and non-contact evidence. Our solver integrates a global-to-local search via 3D FFT correlation over low-discrepancy SO(3) samples, then followed by continuous SE(3) refinement using Lie-algebra updates and analytic contact sensitivities. This pipeline yields efficient exploration and metric-grade convergence without fragile point correspondences. Simulation across free-form meshes achieved sub-0.04 mm and sub-0.4° accuracy and robustness to pose noise and contact loss. On a tooth-preparation robot, our method attained 0.42 mm and 3.75°, outperforming an optical tracker registration while requiring no external sensors. These results demonstrate a practical and precise registration strategy for surgical and industrial robots.

</details>


### [34] [roto 2.0: The Robot Tactile Olympiad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21429)
*Elle Miller,Jayaram Reddy,Ayush Deshmukh,Trevor McInroe,David Abel,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Robot Tactile Olympiad 2.0 (roto 2.0)，一个基于GPU并行的触觉强化学习基准测试平台，支持四种不同机器人形态的盲操作任务，仅依赖本体感知和触觉传感，在Baoding球旋转任务上实现了比现有最先进方法快一个数量级的性能（10秒内旋转13次），并通过开源环境和调优基线降低研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于触觉的强化学习研究存在碎片化问题，且过度集中于饱和的定向任务，缺乏标准化的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建GPU并行化的基准测试平台roto 2.0，覆盖四种不同机器人形态（16自由度到24自由度），采用端到端盲操作范式，仅使用本体感知和触觉传感，无需状态信息或知识蒸馏。

Result: 盲操作智能体在10秒内实现13次Baoding球旋转，性能比当前最先进水平提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过开源环境和稳健调优的基线，降低了触觉强化学习研究的入门门槛，使研究者能够聚焦于基础算法挑战而非繁琐的RL调参工作。

Abstract: Tactile-based reinforcement learning (RL) is currently hindered by fragmented research and a focus on over-saturated orientation tasks. We introduce v2 of the Robot Tactile Olympiad (\texttt{roto 2.0}), a GPU-parallelised benchmark designed to standardise tactile-based RL across four distinct robotic morphologies (16-DOF to 24-DOF). Unlike prior benchmarks, roto focuses on end-to-end "blind" manipulation, utilising only proprioception and tactile sensing without state information or distillation. We demonstrate a significant performance leap, with our blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds, an order of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art speeds. By open-sourcing our environments and robustly tuned baselines, we reduce the barrier to entry and enable researchers to prioritise fundamental algorithmic challenges over tedious RL tuning. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/

</details>


### [35] [HITL-D: Human In The Loop Diffusion Assisted Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21460)
*Riley Zilka,Sergey Khlynovskiy,Allie Wang,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D)共享控制框架，通过扩散策略与人类控制的结合，在多点插入和精细操作任务中减少40%任务完成时间、降低37%感知工作负荷，并提升直觉性和用户信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自主操控系统取得了显著进展，但人类专业知识与基于扩散策略的共享控制之间的整合仍相对未被探索。

Method: 提出HITL-D共享控制框架，利用扩散策略与人类控制的新型组合，根据场景点云和末端执行器笛卡尔位置提供自主的末端执行器姿态更新，从而减少所需的摇杆控制轴数量，降低心理负荷。

Result: 在12名参与者的多任务用户研究中，HITL-D将平均任务完成时间减少40%，感知工作负荷降低37%，并在独立性、直觉性和信心方面的Likert量表评分优于传统远程操作方法。

Conclusion: HITL-D有效整合了人类专业知识与自主辅助，在远程操作的客观和主观指标上均实现了改善。

Abstract: Autonomous manipulation systems have achieved remarkable capabilities, yet the integration of human expertise with diffusion-based policies in shared control remains relatively unexplored. In this paper, we propose Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D), a shared control framework that enhances user performance in multi-step, insertion, and fine manipulation tasks. HITL-D leverages a novel combination of diffusion-based policies and human control to provide autonomous end effector orientation updates conditioned on a scene point cloud and the Cartesian position of the end effector. This approach reduces the number of joystick control axes required, thereby lowering mental workload. In a multi-task user study with 12 participants, HITL-D reduced average task completion times by 40%, decreased perceived workload by 37%, and improved Likert-scale ratings for independence, intuitiveness, and confidence compared to traditional teleoperation methods. These results demonstrate that HITL-D effectively integrates human expertise with autonomous assistance, improving both objective and subjective aspects of teleoperation.

</details>

### [36] [Understanding Deterioration Random Effects for Causal Discovery in Infrastructure Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20400)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合贝叶斯分层风险模型与因果发现的框架，通过GPU加速的NUTS采样估计泵设备的异质性退化率，并利用DirectLiNGAM因果发现揭示不同运行模式对退化速率的影响，实现了从群体平均到异质性感知的预测性维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施退化模型采用群体平均方法，忽略了设备个体间的异质性，导致资产管理决策不够精准。

Abstract: Infrastructure deterioration poses significant challenges for asset management, yet existing approaches rely on population-averaged models that overlook equipment-specific heterogeneity. We then employ DirectLiNGAM to discover causal relationships between 22 engineered time-series features and deterioration rates, stratified by positive ($u_i > 0$, faster deterioration) versus negative ($u_i \leq 0$, slower deterioration) random effects. Analyzing 112 pumps with 92,861 observations over 650 days, we uncover striking heterogeneity: the negative group exhibits causal effects 400$\times$ larger than the positive group, with standard deviation (std) showing a strong positive causal effect ($+1.515$) on deterioration rates in low-risk equipment. We validate linearity assumptions through NonlinearLiNGAM comparison and demonstrate practical scalability through GPU acceleration. Our findings enable targeted maintenance strategies by revealing that different operational regimes require fundamentally distinct management approaches, advancing predictive maintenance from population-averaged to heterogeneity-aware decision making.

Method: 采用Clauset-Shalizi-Newman协议分析比特币尾部相关分布序列，并提出三种原理性时域适配方案。通过UTXO余额和日收益率的分布检验、时域幂律指数对时间原点变动的敏感性分析、残差诊断与尺度不变性检验、九序列跨资产比较（含区块链指标与传统资产）、以及walk-forward Diebold-Mariano滚动预测评估（含十个候选模型）进行综合检验。

Abstract: Bitcoin's price has been described as following a power law (PL) in time, $P \sim t^β$ with $\hatβ\approx 5.7$ over 2010-2026. We test this claim using the Clauset-Shalizi-Newman protocol applied to Bitcoin's tail-relevant distributional series, and develop three principled time-domain adaptations of the protocol. We find that (i) the distributional power law is rejected on UTXO balances and daily |returns|, with lognormal preferred decisively; (ii) the fitted time-domain exponent varies by nearly a factor of three across reasonable shifts of the time origin -- it is not specification-robust in the sense required for a shift-invariant structural reading; (iii) standard residual diagnostics and scale-invariance tests proposed in earlier work cannot distinguish a power law from a multi-component sigmoid stack fit to the same data; (iv) Bitcoin price stands apart in a cross-asset comparison spanning Bitcoin on-chain metrics and traditional asset classes: it is the only series in the nine-series in-sample test where no single-component growth curve improves on the power law, and the quarterly $K=3$ wave-stability bootstrap rejects the PL+AR(1) null on Bitcoin at $p = 0.015$ (strict 15% CV threshold) -- a clear cross-asset separation, although not a Bonferroni-robust rejection; and (v) walk-forward Diebold-Mariano evaluation against ten candidates -- including standard time-series baselines (RW with drift, auto-ARIMA, ETS, local-linear-trend) -- shows the in-sample winner (multi-sigmoid) is among the worst long-horizon forecasters, while the simple power law dominates 12-24 month horizons against every standard baseline at $p < 0.05$, precisely because it does not commit to specific wave shapes. The fit-prediction tradeoff is the practical counterpart of the descriptive findings.

</details>

Abstract: This research note investigates the impact of the experience museum Sensoria, opened in September 2024 in Holzminden, Germany, on local tourism demand and related direct and indirect effects. To this end, the study employs a novel approach by combining causal inference and demand-side economic analysis. A difference-in-differences approach is employed to quantify the number of additional guest overnight stays in the treatment city; the results are converted into industry-specific expenditures, from which the direct and indirect effects of Sensoria are determined. A positive and significant impact which corresponds to 4,691 additional overnight stays can be detected in the first year of operation of the new tourist attraction, resulting in an additional gross turnover of approximately 0.56 million EUR across the hospitality and retail industries and other services. The direct effects and indirect effects amount to approximately 0.23 and 0.21 million EUR, respectively. However, long-term effects cannot (yet) be determined. Additionally, positive effects from small and large events in the cities studied can be demonstrated. This brief study demonstrates that combining the two approaches mentioned holds promise, yet requires a more in-depth analysis, for which suggestions are also discussed regarding how it could be conducted.

Abstract: The Connes-van Suijlekom truncated Weil quadratic form, indexed by a cutoff parameter $c$ that controls the primes $p\leq c$ entering the operator, has a ground state whose Fourier-Mellin zeros provably lie on the critical line; whether they converge to the Riemann zeros as $c\to\infty$ is open (Connes 2026; Connes-Consani-Moscovici 2025). We present, to our knowledge, the first public implementation of the CvS Galerkin matrix at sixteen cutoffs ($c=13$ through $67$, plus $c=100$). Across $c=13$ through $c=67$ at $N=100$, the first-zero absolute error $|γ_1-γ_1^{\mathrm{Riemann}}|$ shrinks monotonically from $\sim 2\times 10^{-55}$ to $\sim 1.5\times 10^{-168}$ -- a 113-OOM convergence across fifteen cutoffs. The smallest-positive even-sector eigenvalue $λ_{\min}^{\mathrm{even}}$ separately reaches $\sim 10^{-334}$ at $c=100$, $N=250$ (275-OOM span from $c=13$), and the same eigenvector recovers $γ_1,\ldots,γ_{10}$ to 307-329 matching digits at $N=250$, $\mathrm{dps}=500$. The raw spectrum at $c=100$ carries 3, 5, 8, 11 negative-sign eigenvalues for $N=100,150,200,250$; continuum positivity of $QW_λ$ is RH-equivalent and we do not assume it at $λ=\sqrt{100}$. We make no claim of proof.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单完备数（unitary perfect numbers）是数论中的一个经典问题，目前仅发现五个例子，第六个是否存在至今未知。本文旨在通过系统性的计算方法缩小搜索范围，将问题归约为一个更精确的分析目标，为最终解决该问题奠定基础。

Method: (1) 将Subbarao-Warren问题中的平衡方程显式保留种子因子2^a+1；(2) 在奇数依赖图中对有界枚举的源核进行分类，识别出两个已知核和五个冒充核；(3) 设计三重过滤器（Zsigmondy型指数障碍、非-3-Higgs见证、2-adic预算超支）消除冒充核；(4) 通过结构引理将H_even归约到素数分支m=2p；(5) 利用因子缓存和APR-CL素性验证证明H_even在前沿区间内的上界；(6) 应用Ford定理获得无条件幂律薄界。

Result: (1) 在1≤a≤10000范围内，所有可能的源核要么是已知的两个核（3²和5⁴），要么是五个已被消除的冒充核；(2) |H_even ∩ [2,40000]| ≤ 201；(3) |H_even ∩ [2,50000]| ≤ 272；(4) 剩余问题归约为Φ_{4p}(2)的除子级问题。

Conclusion: 论文未证明第六个单完备数不存在（即未证明有限性），但完成了有界盒消除、提供了可验证的有限前沿列表，并将问题精确归约为对Φ_{4p}(2)的除子级分析这一明确的分析目标。这为未来彻底解决该问题提供了清晰的路径。

Abstract: A unitary perfect number is a positive integer n satisfying σ^*(n)=2n, where σ^* sums unitary divisors. Only five examples are known, and no sixth has been found. The remaining task is a divisor-level problem for the cyclotomic values Φ_{4p}(2). Thus the paper does not prove finiteness; it gives a bounded-box elimination, a verified finite frontier, and a precise analytic target for closing the remaining branch.

Abstract: We investigate a fifty-year-old conjecture of Erdős and Graham concerning whether the binomial coefficient ${n \choose k}$ with $1 \leq k \leq \frac{n}{2}$ must always have a divisor $\leq n$ that is ``close'' to $n$: that is, bigger than a constant times $n$.
